**Reply**: develop, merge commits (Recommended)

**Reply**: Delete right away (Recommended)

**Reply**: Only multi-unit work (Recommended)

**Options**: Q1: Liquid Glass translucency / foundations only flat / subtle translucent header; Q2: blog plus shared chrome / everything incl. profile layouts; Q3: follow system with manual choice / follow system only / keep sunrise switching; Q4: system fonts on Apple devices + self-hosted Pretendard elsewhere / self-hosted Pretendard everywhere / CDN Pretendard as today

## Human Turn
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T00:27:26Z
**Event**: HUMAN_TURN
**Session**: 2a53a821-c6d3-4f56-935f-36069a2a8404
**Picked**: ["Liquid Glass look","Everything","System + manual choice","Self-hosted Pretendard"]

---

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T00:27:26Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 2a53a821-c6d3-4f56-935f-36069a2a8404
**Question**: Q1. Apple's current guidelines (since 2025) center on Liquid Glass: translucent, layered bars and controls floating over content. The foundations (typography, color, spacing, layout, accessibility) apply to any design; heavy translucency on a website costs some rendering performance and needs solid fallbacks. Which visual direction?
**Reply**: Liquid Glass look

---

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T00:27:26Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 2a53a821-c6d3-4f56-935f-36069a2a8404
**Question**: Q2. Restyling the tokens or the shared header/nav/footer/search triggers the 16-state accessibility review either way. The profile pages (/resume, /portfolio, /portfolio/<role>) also have their own stylesheets, a CSS size budget, markup tests and a print layout tied to the human-reviewed PDF release. Which pages does the redesign cover?
**Reply**: Everything

---

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T00:27:26Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 2a53a821-c6d3-4f56-935f-36069a2a8404
**Question**: Q3. Today the site follows the system light/dark setting only on a first visit; after that it switches at sunrise/sunset using a location prompt and a third-party sunrise API. Apple's guidelines say to respect the system appearance. How should the site choose light or dark?
**Reply**: System + manual choice

---

## Question Replied
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T00:27:26Z
**Event**: QUESTION_REPLIED
**Session**: 2a53a821-c6d3-4f56-935f-36069a2a8404
**Question**: Q4. San Francisco can't be served as a web font (Apple-platform license), but Apple devices already have it via a system font stack (San Francisco + Apple SD Gothic Neo). Pretendard, loaded today from a CDN without integrity hashes, is a Korean font designed to look like Apple's system fonts everywhere; profile pages already self-host a subset. Which fonts?
**Reply**: Self-hosted Pretendard

---

**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T00:27:29Z

**Details**: Q1: Liquid Glass look; Q2: Everything; Q3: System + manual choice; Q4: Self-hosted Pretendard

## Decision Recorded
**Timestamp**: 2026-10-20T14:41:11Z
**Event**: DECISION_RECORDED
**Stage**: build-and-test
**Decision**: Build and Test failed again, on fewer targets: just crossunit, npm run test:e2e and npm audit failed as in run 1, and 4 targets are Not Met and 2 Unverified (22 of 28 Met, up from 18). Root cause: two failures come from generated code. A link inside a highlight drops below 4.5:1 in callouts and table headers (4.07-4.33:1) and to 1.73:1 in the dark homepage note, which loads no highlight style. The open search dialog cuts every result snippet off on one line, at 200% text and at every width. The test vault's deliberate broken link, the pending accessibility review, the missing coverage tool and the pre-existing dependency advisories are not code faults. Candidate fix: loop back to Code Generation, test-first, to make links inside highlights reach 4.5:1 on every ground, style the homepage highlight, let search snippets wrap to two lines, and extend the overflow checks to open dialogs and sheets. Estimated impact: effort, about 1-2 hours plus a fresh Build and Test run; financial cost, none beyond build time; risk, low (search results get a second snippet line, unlike the mockup). Loop-backs used: 1/3. How would you like to proceed?
**Options**: Retry with fix,Accept failure,Abort
